**A:**

You're netting nearly **+12** in total projected points across the board and fixing your biggest weakness. Hit accept before Jamal changes his mind.

**Q:** as I understand it, the period from Veterans Day thru new years is historically a very bullish several weeks. That being said, between S&P, Nasdaq and Russell, which index tends to perform the best during this window

**A:**

→ **Russell 2000** historically leads this post-Veterans/Thanksgiving-to-year-end stretch, averaging roughly **+3.4%** with an **82%** win rate due to year-end small-cap rotation.

→ **Nasdaq Composite** runs second with average gains near **+2.85%** and a ~74.5% win rate, propelled by high-beta tech momentum into the close.

→ **S&P 500** anchors the move with a ~77–80% win rate and average gains around **+2.5%**, offering the steadiest baseline across the broader seasonal window.
